Runtime Binding Families
------------------------
Scheduler runtime bindings and monkey patches grouped by functional family.
safe_to_remove is always false for these binding families.
Classifier rules are deterministic and evaluated in this order:
  1. SCHEDULER_BUILD, REPAIRABLE_*, CODE_CHAIN_* -> build/constants
  2. run_one_step* -> run_one_step
  3. tick* -> tick
  4. create_task* -> create_task
  5. review* -> review
  6. dispatch* -> dispatch
  7. repo* -> repo_state
  8. queue* -> queue
  9. repair* -> repair
  10. path* -> path
  11. plan* or normalize_replan* -> planning
  12. otherwise -> unknown
